### 2026-10-19 — [MINOR] P05-FIX-01: Owner-Reported Paragraph Font Colour/Highlight Defect (DEF-001) — Root-Caused and Fixed

- **Phase:** Phase 05 — Core Modular Editor and Common Content Blocks
- **Status:** **IMPLEMENTED — NOT VERIFIED (native acceptance pending).** The defect is fixed and verified at implementation, interaction and live-browser level; the native Windows colour-picker UI cannot be driven by the agent, so the owner retest remains mandatory and is folded into the still-open `R-010` procedure re-run. `P05-GATE` stays **BLOCKED**, Phase 05 stays **IN PROGRESS**, `R-010` stays **OPEN**, all six exit-criterion checkboxes stay `[ ]`, and DEC-006/DEC-007 are untouched. This is a defect-fix checkpoint, not a phase checkpoint — it closes no exit criterion.
- **Owner-reported failure (recorded as `DEF-001` in the defect register):** in the real Windows app, the paragraph Font Colour and Highlight controls open the colour picker, but the chosen colour is never applied to the selected text — no formatting change in the editor, preview or rendered PDF, and no feedback of any kind. The owner reported this on 2026-10-19 during the first `P05-GATE` walkthrough attempt. No other scenario results were itemized by the owner and none are claimed or inferred here.
- **Branch:** `opencode/p05-fix-rich-text-color-highlight`, created from the verified and pushed `P05-GATE` tip `2cee12a` (not from `main`; `origin/main` untouched at `b56bc59`; tree clean at branch creation). No force-push, no history rewrite, nothing merged to `main`, no pull request.
- **Root cause (three combined causes; empirically reproduced in a managed Chromium browser during this checkpoint — honestly labelled: the exact WebView2 delivery path is inference from the React mechanism plus the live reproduction, not verified inside WebView2):**
  1. **Silent no-format — the owner's symptom.** React's synthetic `onChange` for `type="color"` consults its input value tracker; when the picked value reaches the input through the JavaScript `value` setter the tracker reports "not dirty" and React silently drops the event — the apply handler never ran and no feedback surfaced. Reproduced live: JS-set value + dispatched `input`/`change` → nothing applied, no notice.
  2. **Swatch revert.** The colour inputs carried hard-wired fixed controlled `value`s (`#c0392b` / `#f9e79f`), so React restored the hard-coded value after every pick (observed `v=#c0392b` after a pick) — the choice was never reflected and a wrong colour could be re-applied.
  3. **Fragile target.** The applied range depended on React selection state synced from textarea `select` events; the engine fires such an event during the focus transition to the colour input (observed live on blur), so the applied range was not guaranteed to be the user's chosen selection.
  - Selection itself was preserved in the observed environment (repro: select "beta" [6,10] → a simulated pick applied the colour to "beta" only), so the silent failure points to cause 1, with cause 3 a latent correctness risk.
- **Scope completed:**
  - `src/components/editor-panel/ParagraphContentEditor.tsx` (the only behavioural change): the formatting target is frozen on control activation — `onMouseDown`/`onKeyDown` on the colour labels and the clear buttons capture the current selection before focus moves (`captureTarget()` reading the live textarea selection); a module-level `resolveApplyTarget(frozen ?? non-empty live ?? empty)` always prefers the frozen target, treats an empty frozen target as authoritative (never a stale range) and falls back to a non-empty live selection only when no activation captured one; **native `input`/`change` listeners** (attached once via `useEffect`, reading fresh props through a `latestRef`) are the sole authoritative applying path, immune to React's tracker suppression; React `onChange` only mirrors the swatch display state; `fontColorChoice`/`highlightChoice` state back the swatch values (initially `#c0392b`/`#f9e79f`, reflecting every pick including the last-used colour after a rejected empty-selection apply — Word-like last-used behaviour); focus and selection are restored to the textarea on `change` (and on `applyColor` button success); an empty target surfaces the existing understandable message ("Select some text before changing its colour."); the clear actions (A× / ×) use the same frozen-target path; colour and highlight stay fully independent. `normalizeHexColor`/`applySpanColor` validation is reused from `rich-text.ts`; no new source of truth (document data remains `InlineSpan[]` through `updateParagraphContent`); no editor replacement; B/I/U/S/link untouched (reported working — out of scope).
  - New committed interaction regression suite `verify_p05_fix01.mjs` + `verify:p05-fix01` script (Evidence below).
  - `package.json`: the new script; `jsdom` added as the sole new devDependency (dev-only — the only DOM implementation available for the required component interaction tests; the existing React stack is reused; the real component is compiled to CJS in `node_modules/.cache` per the established verify-script pattern; install reported 0 vulnerabilities).
- **Evidence:**
  - `npm run verify:p05-fix01` → **16 passed, 0 failed**, exit 0. The suite compiles the REAL `ParagraphContentEditor.tsx` (plus the real `rich-text.ts`, `block-content.ts`, `document-file.ts`, `block-actions.ts`, `types/*`) to CJS, renders it with the real React 18 runtime in jsdom, and drives it with DOM events through the real `updateParagraphContent` service, mirroring `App.handleParagraphContentChange` (immutable service → state). It covers all ten required §6 areas: (1) font colour on a selected substring → authoritative spans + preview + control; (2) highlight on a different substring → only that substring; (3) a second colour applies and stays reflected in the control; (4) colour across multiple formatted runs (bold + underline crossing the selection) preserves all unrelated formatting and the text; (5) colour + highlight coexist on the same substring; (6) clearing the colour preserves highlight and other styles (single-mousedown click with a selection-loss replay mid-click — a real click fires mousedown exactly once); (7) clearing the highlight preserves colour; (8) selection-loss/focus transitions and empty-selection behaviour, including the owner's real failure path (JS-setter delivery after a collapsed-selection focus replay still formats the frozen range, control reflects it, focus/selection restored) and the stale-selection guard (collapsed caret → understandable feedback, nothing formatted, no commit); (9) the result committed to the active document and surviving `serializeDocument` → `deserializeDocument` deep-equal; (10) selection alone and an opened-then-cancelled picker never dirty the document (document-snapshot and commit-count equality); plus keyboard (Tab+Enter) activation and the internal/native-delivery engine variant.
  - **Negative control (the suite is not vacuous):** with `ParagraphContentEditor.tsx` reverted to the pre-fix HEAD version, the same suite fails **10 of 16** — including test 8 (the owner's failure path) and the core colour/highlight applies; the fix was then restored (173 insertions / 9 deletions vs HEAD) and the suite re-run green.
  - Full Phase 02–05 battery re-run after the fix: **18 suites = 350 checks, all exit 0** (`verify:p02-m2` 11, `p02-m3` 12, `p02-m4` 9, `p02-m5` 14, `p03-m1` 14, `p03-m2` 18, `p03-m3` 18, `p03-m4` 22, `p03-m5` 17, `p04-m1` 12, `p04-m2` 14, `p04-m3` 18, `p04-m4` 25, `p05-m1` 33, `p05-m2` 27, `p05-m3` 33, `p05-m4` 37 — the 17 prior suites = 334, unchanged from the recorded historical total; + `verify:p05-fix01` 16 = 350; recalculated from this run's actual output).
  - `npx tsc --noEmit` → exit 0; `npm run build` → exit 0 (6.57 s; the pre-existing >500 kB chunk-size warning unchanged).
  - Renderer/fixture integrity re-proven: `verify:p02-m4` 9/9 (incl. the committed P02-M4 baseline compare) and `verify:p04-m3` 18/18 (incl. the gallery-artifact byte-compares) inside the battery; `git diff` empty for `fixtures/` — nothing regenerated, deleted or weakened.
  - **Live walkthrough of the FIXED app** (managed Chromium against the Vite dev server — honestly labelled: not the native Tauri/WebView2 window), §8 all ten steps: paragraph inserted with text "alpha beta gamma"; "beta" coloured through both value-delivery paths including the owner's suppressed-JS-setter path (applied, control reflected `#ff0000`/`#0000ff`, focus+selection restored); "gamma" highlighted independently; colour + highlight coexisting on "beta" (nested preview spans, text intact); a second colour change applied with the highlight kept and the control reflecting `#e74c3c`; A× clear removed the colour and kept the highlight; × clear removed the highlight and kept the colour; an empty-caret pick showed "Select some text before changing its colour." with the spans unchanged (no stale-range apply); text inserted before the styled range remapped the colour +4 characters with the other highlight intact; PoC render succeeded — **2 pages, 3,739 bytes in 78 ms** with the formatted text on page 2. 1 screenshot captured (approved temp location — repository policy keeps screenshots uncommitted).
  - Diff reviewed before commit: exactly `src/components/editor-panel/ParagraphContentEditor.tsx` (173+/9−), `verify_p05_fix01.mjs` (new), `package.json` (script + jsdom devDependency), `package-lock.json` (jsdom tree, all dev), plus this roadmap update; `project_details.md` untouched.
- **Validation performed:** everything in Evidence — the focused suite, the negative control against the actual pre-fix file (git checkout + restore, not a re-implementation), the full battery, `tsc`, build, byte identity, the fixture diff, the live walkthrough, and the pre-commit diff review.
- **Not completed / limitations:**
  - **NOT RUN — the native Windows colour-picker UI itself** (the OS picker through WebView2 and its real value-delivery timing): the agent cannot drive it (P02-M2 precedent); nothing was simulated as native. The owner must retest on Windows: the picker opens, the choice formats the selection and is reflected in the control, preview/PDF show it, and it survives a native Save → Clear → Open round-trip. This is folded into the open `R-010` 6-step procedure (step 1's formatted paragraph now explicitly includes colour/highlight verification through the real picker), followed by the inspection-only `P05-GATE-R1`.
  - The exact WebView2 delivery path for the pre-fix silent failure is inference from the React tracker mechanism plus the managed-Chromium reproduction; it is not verified inside WebView2.
  - The pre-existing notice lifecycle (an error notice persists until a later success path clears it) was observed during the live walkthrough; it is unchanged behaviour, untouched by this fix.
  - B/I/U/S/link were not audited (reported working; out of scope for this fix).
- **Decisions made:** None new — no DEC opened or changed; DEC-006 and DEC-007 untouched; the jsdom devDependency is recorded here and in the board rows as an implementation necessity, not as a DEC.
- **Commit/reference:** single commit on `opencode/p05-fix-rich-text-color-highlight` (fix + regression suite + package files + this roadmap record); its hash is necessarily absent from this entry (a commit cannot contain its own hash) — the exact hash is in the task report.
- **Next action:** the project owner retests on Windows — the native-picker colour/highlight verification plus the `R-010` 6-step procedure re-run; then **`P05-GATE-R1`**, the inspection-only closure pass. Phase 06 must not begin before that. *(Stopping after this checkpoint as instructed.)*

The full 18-suite/350-check battery was NOT re-run — the tree is unchanged at `08cfb24` (the exact `P05-FIX-01` commit; `git status` clean), so that battery's evidence stands as cited; `npx tsc --noEmit` and `npm run build` (both exit 0 at `P05-FIX-01`) likewise stand, no source having changed.
- *Fix present on the branch (source inspection):* `resolveApplyTarget` (frozen target ?? non-empty live selection ?? empty), `captureTarget` on mousedown/keydown of both colour/highlight labels and both clear buttons, native `input`/`change` listeners as the sole authoritative apply path with the `latestRef` fresh-props bridge, and state-backed swatches — all present in the committed `ParagraphContentEditor.tsx`.

**Owner acceptance evidence (recorded exactly as reported; owner-reported, not agent-executed):**

- **Engine fact (measured):** react-pdf **HYPHENATES watermark text too wide for the line box** - a 16-char sentinel at default 72 pt renders as `WM-OFF-SEN-`/`TINEL` across two lines; shorter text renders verbatim. Suite 9.10 uses the short `'WM-OFF'` sentinel.
- Colour is emitted as `scn` (not `rg`); `/ca` is uncompressed in ExtGState; pdfkit splits `TJ` runs at kerning pairs (suite matching is whitespace-insensitive).

### 2026-10-20 - [MINOR] P06-M2: Structural Blocks - Fully Editable Content + Real `PocDocument` Rendering - **VERIFIED** (upgraded 2026-10-21 on owner acceptance, after the `P06-M2-R1` editor-layout corrections; the original status was IMPLEMENTED - NOT VERIFIED)

**Checkpoint:** `P06-M2` - second Phase 06 checkpoint · **Branch:** `opencode/p06-m2-structural-blocks` (from `opencode/p06-m1-dec008-grid-activation` @ `b63b6f9`), pushed to origin, implementation committed as `9cdd9020b48d2784b4a0bedd207ec4443c3f0f6f` · **Status:** **VERIFIED** (owner acceptance recorded 2026-10-21 - the owner's pending acceptance of the rendered structural output was satisfied after the `P06-M2-R1` corrective follow-up fixed three editor-layout defects and the owner personally tested all three corrected editors "working OK"; see the `P06-M2-R1-ACCEPT` acceptance entry appended at the end of this section; the status when written was IMPLEMENTED - NOT VERIFIED per checklist discipline: every checkpoint acceptance criterion had agent-level evidence incl. a live browser walkthrough, but owner acceptance of the rendered structural output - the Phase 06 convention established by `P06-M1` - was still pending).

**Owner acceptance of `P06-M1` / `P06-M1-DEC008` recorded at this checkpoint:** see the acceptance record appended to the `P06-M1-DEC008` entry above - the owner confirmed the rendered header/footer works and root-caused the initial invisibility to "Hide on the first body page" (independent per header/footer); both checkpoints were upgraded to **VERIFIED** on that basis.

**Implemented:**
- **`src/services/structural-block-content.ts` (new)** - immutable action service for all seven structural types. Horizontal rule: thickness range 0.1-12 pt (`HORIZONTAL_LINE_THICKNESS_PT_RANGE`), `normalizeHexColor` colour validation, and the additive `HorizontalLineBlockData.styleSource?: 'theme' | 'block'` policy (schema v1, NO `schemaVersion` bump - absent/`'theme'` keeps the byte-identical theme-token rendering that protects the DEC-008 baselines and the P04-M3 gallery fixtures; the field is set automatically on the first thickness/colour edit, after which the renderer honours the stored values). Section break: optional inline-divider title (empty is valid), never forces a page. Metadata block: ordered selection restricted to the actual `CoverVisibleMetadataField` union (`custom:<id>` must exist; duplicates rejected; labels via `coverFieldLabel`); only ordered keys are stored - values resolve from `doc.metadata` at render time (no stale copies). Revision history: non-empty `revision` required, optional `YYYY-MM-DD` date, stable entry IDs, removal to zero entries allowed (honest empty state); it is manually edited content, explicitly NOT Track Changes (Phase 07 owns snapshots). References: non-empty `key` required, URLs only through `sanitizeHref` (http/https/mailto; cleared by null/''). Exhibit: non-empty `label` required; optional `assetId` must exist in `doc.assets` with an image MIME (NO PDF attachments, no file picking, no new MIME support - P06-M4 owns image import). Page break: deliberately NON-editable structural marker (insert/move/duplicate/delete only). 17 field-scoped error codes; `MAX_STRUCTURAL_TEXT_LENGTH` 4000; no-op patches return the same document reference; `modifiedAt` stamped only on real changes; validation runs before commit; single `applyStructuralAction` dispatcher.
- **`src/components/editor-panel/StructuralContentEditor.tsx` (new)** + **`EditorPanel.tsx` / `Workspace.tsx` / `App.tsx` wiring** - seven editors (HorizontalLineEditor, SectionBreakEditor, PageBreakMarker, MetadataBlockEditor, RevisionHistoryEditor, ReferenceEditor, ExhibitEditor) behind one `onStructuralAction` prop routed through App's `handleStructuralAction`; `EditorPanel` gained optional `customFields`/`assets` props (default `[]`); existing panel CSS classes reused with additive `.structural-field-*`/`.structural-entry-*` rules in `EditorPanel.css`.
- **`src/renderer/poc/poc-document.tsx`** - real renderers replacing every "[block type not mapped]" fallback for the structural types: `HorizontalRuleBlock` (theme tokens when unstyled, stored thickness/colour when block-authored), section break (inline divider + optional title), `MetadataBlock` (rows resolved from `doc.metadata` at render time), `RevisionHistoryBlock` (manually edited table), `ReferenceBlock` (wrapping citations; links re-checked through `sanitizeHref` at render time), `ExhibitBlock` (label/title/notes + optional image asset; honest "[Referenced exhibit asset is missing...]" note for dangling references); `forceBreak` implements the page-break chain collapse (a break applies to the following block only when a non-page-break block precedes it; leading, consecutive and trailing breaks produce at most ONE boundary, so no unintended empty pages; markers are never removed - a deliberate blank page is an empty paragraph between two breaks); font size/body theme/monochrome respected; DEC-006 evidence unaffected.
- **`src/services/block-registry.ts`** - page-break/section-break descriptions and structural summaries made self-explanatory ("page break - next block starts on a new page" vs "section break (inline divider - no new page)"; horizontal rule shows theme vs block-styled); `verify_p05_m1.mjs` assertion updated to the new page-break summary wording (legitimate P06-M2-motivated update; suite still 33/33).
- **`src/renderer/poc/poc-node-entry.ts`** - additive exports for the structural service, `createBlock` and `insertBlock` (consumed by the committed suite).
- **`src/types/blocks.ts`** - additive optional `styleSource` field on `HorizontalLineBlockData`.
- **`verify_p06_m2.mjs` (new, `npm run verify:p06-m2`)** - 37 checks: service semantics (immutability, no-op same-reference identity, `modifiedAt` stamping, field-scoped validation incl. thickness range/colour/metadata-field rejection/duplicate fields/revision date format/unsafe URL/missing exhibit asset), horizontal-rule theme-vs-block policy incl. byte-identity of theme-path output, page-break semantics (mid-chain, leading, consecutive, trailing, no marker text) and section-break inline semantics on real rendered PDFs, metadata ordering/render-time resolution/empty states, revision add/edit/remove/stable IDs, references order/sanitize/wrapping/`/URI` link annotation, exhibit label/`/Subtype /Image` XObject/dangling-asset note, representative-document render incl. determinism and a fixtures-untouched assertion.

**Decisions (agent-level; no owner decision required):**
- `styleSource` as an additive optional field rather than a schema bump or a behaviour change - protects every committed baseline and the gallery fixtures while letting users author rule styles.
- Page-break collapse semantics (leading/consecutive/trailing = at most one boundary) over literal marker-per-break rendering, to avoid unintended blank pages; markers are never deleted so user intent is preserved and reversible.
- Revision history is manually edited content only - no fabricated data, no automatic snapshots (Track Changes/audit snapshots stay Phase 07 scope).
- Exhibits reference optional existing image assets only - no attachments, no file picking, no new MIME types (P06-M4 owns image import).

### 2026-10-21 - [MINOR] P06-M2-R1: Structural Editor Layout Corrections (owner-reported usability defects in the shipped P06-M2 editors) - **VERIFIED** (upgraded 2026-10-21 on owner acceptance; the original status was IMPLEMENTED - NOT VERIFIED)

**Checkpoint:** `P06-M2-R1` - P06-M2 corrective follow-up (NOT a new-phase checkpoint; `P06-M3` remains NOT STARTED) · **Branch:** `opencode/p06-m2-structural-editor-fixes` (from `opencode/p06-m2-structural-blocks` @ `5297fc3`), pushed to origin, implementation committed as `1d21822` (editors + CSS + suite), this roadmap-only commit its direct child · **Status:** **VERIFIED** (owner acceptance recorded 2026-10-21 - the owner personally tested all three corrected editors and confirmed "working OK"; see the `P06-M2-R1-ACCEPT` acceptance entry appended at the end of this section; the status when written was IMPLEMENTED - NOT VERIFIED per checklist discipline: agent-level evidence was complete - automated suite + measured live browser walkthrough + screenshots - but owner/human verification of the corrected metadata, references and exhibit editors was still pending; on that acceptance `P06-M2` was upgraded in the same record).

**Trigger:** the project owner tested the shipped P06-M2 structural editors and reported three usability defects:
1. **Metadata block** - "only Title, Document ID and Document Status are available. Other relevant document metadata cannot be selected. If a selected field is removed, it cannot be added again."
2. **References** - "the Add Reference button is obscured by the first citation, preventing further citations from being added."
3. **Exhibit / annexure** - "its editor layout extends horizontally outside the block and is difficult to use." (label, title, notes, asset behaviour themselves accepted as working)

**Root causes (established by code inspection + live DOM measurement at a 1296x886 viewport, not guessed):**
- ALL structural editors rooted their JSX in `<div className="content-field">`, whose CSS is `display: flex` (a HORIZONTAL row). The editor's title field, hint, entry list and Add button therefore sat SIDE-BY-SIDE instead of stacked. Measured overflow (card box vs content scroll width): metadata 396 px box / 706 px content; references 396 / 717; exhibit 396 / 1144 (its Notes textarea squeezed to 22 px). The overflowing content was CLIPPED by `.editor-scroll-area { overflow-x: hidden }`.
- **Metadata (defect 1):** with the selected-fields list occupying the card's visible width, the entire "Add a field" list rendered from x=804 while the card ended at x=746 - EVERY add button in the list was fully invisible (the whole list sat past the card edge, measured). The owner therefore saw only the three default fields, and a removed field (re-offered only in that invisible list) could not be found again. The picker itself was and remains COMPLETE (all 22 scalar `METADATA_FIELD_SPECS` fields + `createdAt`/`modifiedAt` + document custom fields - verified); the restricted APPEARANCE was purely the clipped layout. The prior agent-side walkthrough missed it because programmatic DOM clicks ignore clipping.
- **References (defect 2):** the "Add reference" button was the LAST child of the horizontal row - pushed into the clipped zone as a 26 px sliver (x=1027, beyond the card's right edge at x=746), so the first citation effectively covered it.
- **Exhibit (defect 3):** the same horizontal root compounded with per-field `content-field` rows (label + control side by side; nowrap labels) stretched the form to 1144 px inside the 396 px card.
- Same defect class (closely related root causes, fixed in the same pass): the revision editor shared the horizontal root (its "Add revision entry" button sat beside the entry list), and the shared `.block-card-main`/`.block-card-actions` card header (select + up/down/Duplicate/Delete) overflowed the card by ~23 px at narrow panel widths.

**Fixes (editor + CSS only; document model, schema and renderer behaviour UNTOUCHED):**
- **`src/components/editor-panel/StructuralContentEditor.tsx`** - the metadata, revision, references and exhibit editors now root in a new VERTICAL stack class `structural-editor`; every label+control pair inside them uses `content-field content-field--stack` (label above control); the metadata "Add a field" section gained an honest hint ("Every field listed here exists in the document model and can be added, removed and added again. A selected field whose value is empty in Document Information is omitted from the rendered output until you fill that value in."). The horizontal-rule, section-break and page-break editors were NOT structurally changed (owner-accepted; their `content-row` roots already wrap).
- **`src/components/editor-panel/EditorPanel.css`** - new `.structural-editor` (column, `min-width: 0`) and `.content-field--stack` (column, `flex: 1`, `min-width: 0`, full-width controls that can shrink) rules inside the existing P06-M2 structural section; `.block-card-main` and `.block-card-actions` gained `flex-wrap: wrap` so the card header wraps instead of overflowing at narrow widths. No hard-coded widths; no absolute positioning; no new dependencies.
- **`verify_p06_m2.mjs`** - the suite now compiles `StructuralContentEditor.tsx` and renders the REAL editor components with `react-dom/server` (no source-text grepping for structure): new group 10 (six checks) asserts the metadata editor offers every supported field as a visible add option (incl. subtitle, organization/laboratory name, issue number/date, document holder, created/modified dates, customs), a removed field round-trips (add -> remove -> re-add with order preserved, identical selection = same-reference no-op), the references/exhibit/revision editors root in `structural-editor` with Add buttons in vertical order after their lists, and the CSS defines the vertical stack with no editor reintroducing the horizontal root. Existing test 9.3 strengthened with the task-relevant scalar keys. NO renderer test changed - rendered-output behaviour is untouched by this follow-up.

**Evidence:**
- **`npm run verify:p06-m2` 43/43** (37 prior + 6 new corrective checks), exit 0.
- **Full regression in NORMAL (non-regeneration) mode - all 20 suites exit 0** (`verify_p02_m2` 11, `verify_p02_m3` 12, `verify_p02_m4` 10, `verify_p02_m5` 14, `verify_p03_m1` 14, `verify_p03_m2` 18, `verify_p03_m3` 18, `verify_p03_m4` 22, `verify_p03_m5` 17, `verify_p04_m1` 12, `verify_p04_m2` 14, `verify_p04_m3` 18, `verify_p04_m4` 25, `verify_p05_fix01` 16, `verify_p05_m1` 33, `verify_p05_m2` 27, `verify_p05_m3` 33, `verify_p05_m4` 37, `verify_p06_m1` 63, `verify_p06_m2` 43 -> **457 checks total, 0 failures**; working tree unchanged by the runs; DEC-008 baselines byte-identical - no fixture was written or regenerated).
- `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0 (strict app flags and the non-strict flags the older suites invoke); `npm run build` exit 0.
- **Live browser walkthrough (Vite dev @ localhost:5173, stopped after; tab closed):**
  - *Metadata (defect 1):* card overflow 706 px -> **0 px**; all 19 add buttons measured inside the visible card (previously all clipped); owner flow exercised - add Subtitle, add Organization / Laboratory Name, remove Subtitle, confirm it is re-offered AND visibly inside the card (button box 715-741 within card 348-746), re-add it; final selection order Title, Document ID / Code, Document Status, Organization / Laboratory Name, Subtitle.
  - *References (defect 2):* card overflow 717 px -> **0 px**; "Add reference" is a full-card-width button inside the card below the citation list (previously a 26 px clipped sliver outside it); added citations sequentially to 3 entries with the button reachable each time, edited entries, deleted one (2 remain), and a `javascript:alert(1)` URL was REJECTED with the visible notice "That URL is not a safe link (only http, https and mailto links are accepted)." - sanitization behaviour unchanged.
  - *Exhibit (defect 3):* card overflow 1144 px -> **0 px** at the natural viewport; Notes textarea 22 px -> 180 px -> full card width after the stack refinement; label/title/notes committed; the honest "(no asset attached)" state and the P06-M4 note intact.
  - *Narrow-viewport verification (recorded sizes):* the browser viewport could not be resized directly, so narrower windows were emulated by constraining `.workspace` width (its `minmax(...fr)` tracks reflow exactly as at a narrower window): at **1024 px** (grid 260/360/404) and at **940 px with the app's real `@media (max-width: 1024px)` track floors 240/320/360** (grid 240/326.7/373.3 - the narrowest layout the app's own responsive rules produce), all four structural editors measured **0 px overflow with zero elements outside the card** (metadata add-list fully visible; Add reference / Add revision entry full-width; exhibit Notes and asset select full-width). At the natural **1296x886** viewport all four also measure 0 px.
  - *Rendering regression check:* re-rendered the active document (cover + structural blocks) - "Rendered the active document (5,222 bytes in 120 ms)"; the preview text layer contains the metadata rows, both citations with the link ONLY on the safe URL (no `javascript:` text anywhere), the exhibit label/title/notes and the committed revision description, with NO fallback markers - editor-only fixes did not regress structural rendering.
  - Screenshots captured for all three corrected editors (metadata add-list, references with the visible Add reference button, exhibit vertical form).
  - Harness note (not a product defect): the walkthrough automation raced two rapid block inserts, creating a duplicate revision block; it was removed through the normal delete-confirmation flow (which itself exercised the corrected layout).

**Unchanged / preserved:** `project_details.md` untouched; document schema stays v1 (no `schemaVersion` bump, no migration - the metadata block still stores field keys only and resolves values from `doc.metadata` at render time); the structural service was NOT modified (its validation, ordering, duplicate rejection and no-op identity semantics already met the requirements); rendered output behaviour, header/footer, watermark, DEC-006 gallery-only scope, DEC-007 and the resolved DEC-008 records and baselines all untouched; normal test runs wrote no artifacts.

**Known limitations of this follow-up:** narrow-width emulation was performed by container-width constraining (the harness browser window itself is fixed at 1296x886) - the emulated grid tracks match the app's own responsive minimums, but an owner check on a real narrow window is still welcome; the pre-P06 metadata-renderer omission policy (selected fields with empty document values are omitted from the rendered output, cover-page policy) is unchanged - the new editor hint explains it instead.

**Scope:** exactly the three owner-reported defects + the same-defect-class root causes above; no P06-M3 work; `P06-M2` stays **IMPLEMENTED - NOT VERIFIED** pending owner acceptance of these corrections; Phase 06 stays **IN PROGRESS**, exit-criterion checkboxes stay `[ ]`.

Historical entries were preserved unchanged.

**Checkpoint:** `P06-M3` - formula blocks (§6.3 / spec §10.6) · **Branch:** `opencode/p06-m2-structural-editor-fixes` (continued from `155693d`), pushed to origin, implementation committed as `09d36b5` (engine + service + editor + renderer + planner + suite + vite define fix), this roadmap-only commit its direct child · **Status:** **IMPLEMENTED - NOT VERIFIED** (agent-level evidence is complete - committed 43-check suite + full 21-suite regression + tsc/build + a live browser walkthrough with rendered-PDF proof; owner/human verification of the rendered formula output is pending, per the checklist discipline that every checkpoint starts IMPLEMENTED - NOT VERIFIED; Phase 06 stays IN PROGRESS with exit-criterion checkboxes `[ ]` and `P06-M4`/`P06-M5` stay NOT STARTED).

**Scope:** exactly P06-M3 (formula blocks). No P06-M4+ work; no document schema version bump (formula block data was already in schema v1 - service/renderer/editor are additive); `project_details.md` and `AGENTS.md` untouched; DEC-006/DEC-007 stay OPEN, DEC-008 stays RESOLVED; no fixtures/baselines regenerated and `UPDATE_ARTIFACTS=1` was never used; no unrelated refactors.

**What was implemented:**

- **`src/services/math-engine.ts` (new)** - the LaTeX engine: `mathjax-full` 3.2.1 with the `base`/`ams`/`cases`/`newcommand`/`boldsymbol` TeX packages loaded by side-effect config imports (`noundefined`/`autoload`/`require` deliberately excluded), a fresh MathJax document per conversion with pinned `em=16, ex=8` for cross-machine determinism, memoization (cap 400 entries), and `renderLatexToMathSvg`/`parse` helpers that NEVER throw (errors surface as structured results; error detection via the `data-mjx-error` attribute). `validateLatex` returns "the formula is empty (no LaTeX content)" for empty/whitespace input and the exact MathJax message (e.g. "Missing argument for \frac") for invalid input; `estimateTextWidthPt` provides the width estimate the planner/renderer share.
- **`src/services/formula-content.ts` (new)** - immutable `applyFormulaAction` dispatcher over the `formula` block: set/clear LaTeX (validated through the engine), display mode, alignment (left/center/right), equation number (set/empty), "Where:" label override (set/empty), and add/update/remove/move of variable-definition entries (symbol/definition/unit with per-field limits); same-reference no-ops, `modifiedAt` stamped only on real changes, field-specific error codes/messages, validation before commit. Conventions match `structural-block-content.ts` (incl. the `=== false` narrowing style so the legacy non-strict suites compile).
- **`src/renderer/poc/poc-math-svg.tsx` (new)** - MathJax SVG string -> react-pdf vector primitives: `parseMathSvgDocument` (viewBox, `<use>`/`<g>`/`<path>`/`<rect>`/`<line>`/`<svg>`), `expandMathPathData` (relative -> absolute, `Q`/`T` -> exact cubics with the 2/3 control-point rule, `S` -> `C`, `H`/`V` -> `L`, emitting only `M L C S Z`; any unhandled command falls back to the original data), and `mathSvgToReact` with a memo table. Formulas are therefore crisp VECTOR PATHS at any zoom - deliberately, since pdfkit text cannot typeset math.
- **`src/renderer/poc/formula-layout.ts` (new)** - the pure `planFormulaLayout` planner: uniform scale-to-fit (never enlarges), beside/below decision for the Where list (beside iff `formula + cell + gap + whereNatural <= content`), gaps (`FORMULA_WHERE_GAP_PT` 14, `FORMULA_NUMBER_GAP_PT` 10, `FORMULA_SYMBOL_GAP_PT` 8), below-mode `whereWidth = contentWidth` with gap 0, and the honest `scaledToFit` flag.
- **`src/renderer/poc/poc-document.tsx`** - `FormulaBlock` renderer wired into `BlockRenderer` (`case 'formula'`): font size is ALWAYS the document base size (`resolveBodyTheme`... via tokens - no independent formula-size setting), display mode selects MathJax display styling plus vertical breathing room, alignment applies inside the band left of the number cell, the optional number is pinned right-aligned at the right edge, the Where list renders BESIDE (right of) the formula when the planner says there is room and BELOW otherwise (note: below-mode list renders AFTER the Where label, PDF y of note < y of label), and `[Formula scaled to fit the page width]` prints when scaled. The engine runs once per LaTeX string (memoized), so re-renders are cheap.
- **`src/components/editor-panel/FormulaContentEditor.tsx` (new)** + **`EditorPanel.tsx`/`EditorPanel.css`/`Workspace.tsx`/`App.tsx` wiring** - the content editor (props `{blockId, data, onAction, onError}`, matching the structural editors): live LaTeX textarea with real-time parse hint (`content-derived-hint is-invalid` + exact MathJax error while typing, valid hint otherwise), display-mode checkbox, alignment select, equation-number and Where-label fields (blur-commit, empty clears), and variable-definition cards (add/update/remove with the existing service messages surfaced through the panel notice). App gains `handleFormulaAction` (immutable service, `setActiveDocument` only when `changed`).
- **`vite.config.ts`** - MathJax's `js/components/version.js` contains an EAGER `eval('require')` guard reachable from `js/handlers/html.js`; the white screen found in the live walkthrough ("ReferenceError: require is not defined") was fixed the way MathJax's own bundlers do it: `define: { PACKAGE_VERSION }` injected at top level AND in `optimizeDeps.esbuildOptions.define` (version read from the installed `node_modules/mathjax-full/package.json`). Node-based suites never see this path (native CJS).
- **`verify_p06_m3.mjs` (new, `npm run verify:p06-m3`)** + **`poc-node-entry.ts` additive exports** + **`package.json`** (`mathjax-full` dependency, script). 43 checks: engine (determinism, empty/error messages, package list, no-throw on hostile input), service algebra (set/clear per field, variable add/update/remove/move, no-op same-reference, modifiedAt stamping, error codes), SVG conversion + `expandMathPathData` command-by-command, planner boundary cases (beside/below threshold with 0.1 pt probes), real-component markup (editor renders from real data through react-dom/server), and live PDF geometry + persistence round-trip through the committed fixtures.

**Suite evidence:** `npm run verify:p06-m3` -> **43 passed, 0 failed, exit 0**. Six suite-side fixes were made during bring-up (uppercase react-pdf primitive tags in markup assertions + a quiet wrapper for the react-dom/server casing warnings; `contentWidthPt: 300` and 0.1 pt boundary probes in the planner checks; `deserializeDocument` takes the JSON string; the pdfjs below-mode needle accounts for pdfkit hyphenation; the Workspace import path; a custom-genId assertion; one tautology removed) - all suite-side, none weakening product checks.

**Regression evidence:** the narrowing-style fixes (`=== false`/`=== true` in `math-engine.ts`, `formula-content.ts`, `FormulaContentEditor.tsx`, `FormulaBlock`) restored 7 previously-failing non-strict suites (p02-m4, p04-m2/3/4, p05-m2/3/4) and are behavior-identical; ALL 21 suites were then (re-)run against the final tree: p02-m2 11, p02-m3 12, p02-m4 10, p02-m5 14, p03-m1 14, p03-m2 18, p03-m3 18, p03-m4 22, p03-m5 17, p04-m1 12, p04-m2 14, p04-m3 18, p04-m4 25, p05-fix01 16, p05-m1 33, p05-m2 27, p05-m3 33, p05-m4 37, p06-m1 63, p06-m2 43, p06-m3 43 = **500 checks, all exit 0** (NORMAL mode, tree unchanged by the runs). `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0; `npm run build` exit 0 (8.19 s after the vite.config change; the chunk-size warning is pre-existing).

**Live browser walkthrough (Vite dev @ localhost:5173, stopped afterwards):** the app initially white-screened with "ReferenceError: require is not defined" (MathJax version.js - fixed by the `define` above; after the fix: zero console errors); inserted a Formula block from the Type dropdown -> the editor mounted with live hint, display-mode checked, Center alignment and the honest empty variable-definitions state; typing `\frac{a}` showed the live red hint "Cannot be rendered: Missing argument for \frac" (`content-derived-hint is-invalid` class confirmed); typing the SIGMA formula returned the valid hint; committed `(3)`, the custom "Where (definitions):" label and one variable card (`\sigma` / mg/kg / sample standard deviation) through blur-commits - the block summary updated to "LaTeX display — \sigma = \sqrt{\frac{1}{N-1}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(x_i-\bar{x})..." proving the App dispatcher stored the data; a stray second card was removed live (remove dispatch); "Rendered the active document (9,069 bytes in 116 ms)" -> 2-page preview whose body page shows the typeset vector formula (sigma/sqrt/sum/bar glyphs) centered in display mode, "(3)" pinned at the right edge, the bold custom Where label and "sample standard deviation (mg/kg)" BESIDE the formula; screenshots captured throughout.

**Known limitations (deliberate, recorded):** formulas are vector PATHS in the PDF - not selectable/searchable text (MathJax SVG is the only reliable in-v1.0 math typesetter; a text-mode fallback was rejected as dishonest output); only the TeX subset the five enabled packages parse (no `require`, no `autoload`); no independent formula font-size setting (spec: tied to base typography); mid-block page-crossing shares the P06-M2 limitation (a very tall formula/Where stack is not split across pages - it scales to width and flows as one unit); Where-label bold is rendered visually and asserted only where the pdfjs text layer proves it; native Tauri dialogs NOT RUN (P02-M2 precedent); the react-pdf primitive-casing warnings in react-dom/server markup are a suite-side suppression, not a product concern.

**Deviations from the plan:** none from the roadmap scope. Two engineering discoveries worth noting: (1) the Vite `PACKAGE_VERSION` define (bundler hook required by mathjax-full - the live white screen); (2) false-branch truthiness narrowing on the `ok` discriminants fails under the legacy suites' non-strict tsc, so the P06-M3 files use the explicit `=== false`/`=== true` style that already exists in `rich-text.ts`/`list-callout-content.ts` (behavior-identical; the 43-check suite compiles under `--strict` and passes either way).

**Commits:** implementation `09d36b5` on `opencode/p06-m2-structural-editor-fixes` (15 files: 6 new + 9 modified, incl. `vite.config.ts`); this roadmap-only commit its direct child (its hash is in the task report). `main` untouched; nothing pushed except this feature branch.

**Next -> `P06-M4`** (image and signature blocks) - NOT STARTED; requires explicit owner instruction. Owner acceptance review of the rendered formula output may also be requested first.

*(Stopping after this task as instructed.)*

---

**Checkpoint:** `P06-M3-R1` - formula alignment correction (NOT a new-phase checkpoint; a corrective follow-up to `P06-M3`) · **Branch:** `opencode/p06-m2-structural-editor-fixes` (from `be95035`), implementation committed as `a1662b7` (renderer fix + 3 regression tests), this roadmap-only commit its direct child · **Status:** **IMPLEMENTED - NOT VERIFIED** (agent-level verification complete - the defect was reproduced with real PDF geometry, fixed, and re-verified through pdfjs operator-list measurements plus the full check matrix; the owner must re-test left, centre and right alignment in the actual application and rendered PDF before this checkpoint - and `P06-M3` - can be considered VERIFIED; Phase 06 stays IN PROGRESS, `P06-M4`/`P06-M5` stay NOT STARTED).

**Owner-reported defect (P06-M3 acceptance review, 2026-10-21):** of the nine acceptance areas, eight were confirmed working (formula entry/editing, invalid-LaTeX validation, mathematical notation, equation numbering, Where section, variable definitions, page fitting/scaling notice, regression safety) and **Display and alignment FAILED**: "Everything is left-aligned, even when centre or right alignment is selected." The acceptance result is preserved verbatim in the P06-M3 records above - `P06-M3` remains **IMPLEMENTED - NOT VERIFIED** because its acceptance is incomplete.

**Diagnosis (before any edit):** the alignment VALUE flows correctly end-to-end - the editor's Alignment select dispatches `{hAlign}` (FormulaContentEditor), the `formula-content` service validates and persists it (`invalid-halign` code, `FORMULA_ALIGNMENTS`), the registry default is `center`, and `FormulaBlock` reads `block.hAlign`. The defect was in the rendering layer: `FormulaBlock` applied the alignment with **`justifyContent`** on the formula cell - but that cell is a COLUMN-direction flex container whose height equals its content, so the main-axis (vertical) property had NO effect and the MathJax SVG box always sat at the horizontal cross-axis start (left). Reproduced with real PDF geometry first (a throwaway probe, deleted before commit): rendering `E = mc^2` at all three alignments through the real service produced IDENTICAL pdfjs operator-list path origins (x = 70.87 = the left margin, to 0.01 pt), with and without an equation number.

**Corrective implementation:** one property in `FormulaBlock` (`src/renderer/poc/poc-document.tsx`): the formula cell now applies **`alignItems`** (the cross-axis property) with the same `flex-start` / `center` / `flex-end` mapping against the planner's `formulaCellWidthPt` band, with a root-cause comment. Nothing else changed: the right-pinned equation-number cell (its own `alignItems: flex-end` cell), the Where beside/below planner and column, base-typography font sizing, vector-path rendering and the honest scale-to-fit note are untouched. After the fix the measured box origins match the planner arithmetic to < 0.1 pt: left = margin (70.87), centre = margin + (cell − width)/2 (276.49), right = margin + cell − width (482.12); with an equation number the band correctly excludes the number cell (centre/right shift left by half/full of its 22.1 pt width) and the number stays pinned at the content edge.

**Files changed:** `src/renderer/poc/poc-document.tsx` (1 property + comment) and `verify_p06_m3.mjs` (+271 lines: the `pathOrigins` pdfjs operator-list CTM helper, the `alignDoc`/`expectedAlignXs` fixtures mirroring the renderer's exact width policy, tests 8.5-8.7, header note). No service, editor, schema, fixture or baseline was touched; no dependency changes; no `UPDATE_ARTIFACTS=1`.

**Regression tests added (would fail against the defective implementation):**
- **8.5** the three alignments move the REAL formula box - left/centre/right origins measured through the pdfjs operator-list CTM (save/restore stack + transform concat) and asserted against planner arithmetic, then asserted strictly distinct and ordered;
- **8.6** alignment x the right-pinned equation number - the formula aligns inside the REDUCED band while the number's right edge stays at the area edge, alignment-invariant to < 0.5 pt;
- **8.7** alignment x the Where list - the beside column stays fixed while the formula moves (all three alignments), and in below mode (90 mm margins) the full-width list stays at the left margin while the formula right-aligns. (Below-mode and beside checks measure the DOMINANT glyph cluster because the Where symbol is itself a small vector SVG that leftmost-ink measurement would conflate.)

**Evidence of test strength:** with the fix temporarily stashed (defective renderer restored via `git stash`), the suite ran **43 passed, 3 failed** (8.5-8.7 fail) - proving the new tests catch the defect; with the fix restored, **46 passed, 0 failed, exit 0**.

**Full check matrix (actual results, final tree):** `npm run verify:p06-m3` 46/46 exit 0; full regression - all 21 suites, **503 checks, every exit 0** in NORMAL mode (p02-m2 11, p02-m3 12, p02-m4 10, p02-m5 14, p03-m1 14, p03-m2 18, p03-m3 18, p03-m4 22, p03-m5 17, p04-m1 12, p04-m2 14, p04-m3 18, p04-m4 25, p05-fix01 16, p05-m1 33, p05-m2 27, p05-m3 33, p05-m4 37, p06-m1 63, p06-m2 43, p06-m3 46); `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0; `npm run build` exit 0 (9.24 s, pre-existing chunk-size warning only). No fixture or baseline changed (`git status` showed only the two authorized files; baselines protected - the suite renders in memory only).

**Known limitations / notes:** the fix is renderer-only and does not change what alignment MEANS in the spec (the formula aligns within the band left of its equation number, which keeps its own right-aligned cell); formulas remain vector paths (not selectable text) as recorded at P06-M3; the operator-list CTM measurement asserts against a FIXED short fixture formula (`E = mc^2`) - a future MathJax structural change that moved the dominant glyph cluster would fail loudly, not silently; native Tauri dialogs remain NOT RUN (irrelevant to this renderer-only fix); no live-browser re-walkthrough was performed for this correction - the owner's re-test in the real application is the required acceptance step.

**Deviations from the plan:** none. Scope stayed at the one confirmed defect plus its regression tests; no P06-M4 work; no schema, dependency, fixture, or DEC changes (DEC-006/DEC-007 OPEN, DEC-008 RESOLVED, all untouched).

**Commits:** implementation `a1662b7` on `opencode/p06-m2-structural-editor-fixes` (2 files: renderer fix + suite tests); this roadmap-only commit its direct child (its hash is in the task report). `main` untouched; normal push of this feature branch only.

**Next -> owner re-acceptance:** the owner should test left, centre and right alignment in the actual DocStudio application and rendered PDF (with and without an equation number, with a Where list). Only on that acceptance can `P06-M3-R1` and `P06-M3` be upgraded to VERIFIED; `P06-M4` (image and signature blocks) remains NOT STARTED and requires explicit owner instruction.

*(Stopping after this task as instructed.)*
